{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_8/projects/acr-vla-conformal-refinement/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Project Memory\\n\\n## 失败模式记录\\n\\n- [失败模式] Cron false positive：eval 脚本不写 W&B 也不写 sentinel file，进程正常退出后 cron 5 次检测找不到标记 → 自动标记为 failed。受影响：eval-vla-only-long-seed46, eval-vla-only-goal-seed46。修复方案：tail 日志确认实际完成 → 手动修正 conclusion=success + 补录 metrics。预防：eval 实验应检查 output JSON 是否存在作为 sentinel。\\n\\n- [失败模式] FMVD 信号无效：ODE velocity dispersion 与 K=10 action variance 几乎无相关性 (ρ=0.0825, 远低于 0.3 阈值)。根因：flow matching 的确定性 ODE 轨迹的 velocity norm 变异编码的是 denoising dynamics，而非 action-level uncertainty。per-task 方向不一致 (milk ρ=-0.284)。FMVD 分布极窄 (std=0.040)，无法区分 uncertain vs confident steps。**教训：zero-cost signal 不能假设有效——denoising 空间的统计量不一定映射到 action 空间的 uncertainty。K=2 L2 divergence (直接在 action 空间测量) 是更直觉的替代方案。**\\n\\n- [失败模式] OpenVLA-7B eval timeout：OpenVLA-7B 每 episode ~77s（SmolVLA ~30s），500 episodes 需 ~10.7h。默认 timeout_hours=6 不够 → EXIT_CODE=124。修复方案：OpenVLA eval 实验 timeout_hours 至少设 12h。\\n\\n## LIBERO VLA-only Baselines (SmolVLA, K=1)\\n\\n### LIBERO-Goal (n=5)\\n| Seed | SR |\\n|------|------|\\n| seed42 | 71% |\\n| seed43 | 69% |\\n| seed44 | 73% |\\n| seed45 | 63% |\\n| seed46 | 71% |\\n| **Mean±Std** | **69.4%±3.8%** |\\n\\n### LIBERO-10 / Long (n=5)\\n| Seed | SR |\\n|------|------|\\n| seed42 | 11% |\\n| seed43 | 13% |\\n| seed44 | 12% |\\n| seed45 | 17% |\\n| seed46 | 18% |\\n| **Mean±Std** | **14.2%±3.1%** |\\n\\n## Spatial obs_only CRM (ε=0.2, K=10) Cross-seed Results (n=6)\\n\\n| Seed | SR |\\n|------|------|\\n| seed42 | 35% |\\n| seed43 | 34% |\\n| seed44 | 36% |\\n| seed45 | 34% |\\n| seed46 | 33% |\\n| seed47 | 32% |\\n| **Mean±Std** | **34.0%±1.4%** |\\n\\nSpatial VLA-only baseline: 29.3%±1.7% (n=4). CRM K=10 提升 ~4.7pp，统计显著。\\n\\n**观察**:\\n- 强二分性：3 个 task ≥80%，4 个 task 0%——CRM 修正对某些空间关系有效（next_to, between），对其他无效（on_stove, on_cabinet, on_ramekin）\\n- 0% task 跨 seed 稳定为 0%，说明这不是随机波动而是 CRM 在这些 task 上的系统性局限\\n\\n## CRM Coverage 限制\\n\\nCRM v3 训练数据 = Object(10) + Spatial(10) = 20 tasks。LIBERO-Goal 和 LIBERO-10 (Long) 的 tasks 不在训练覆盖范围内。CRM-Goal 和 CRM-Long 实验是 OOD 泛化测试——Director 已知并批准。\\n\\n## Adaptive K 信号探索\\n\\n| 信号 | ρ vs K10_var | 结论 | 备注 |\\n|------|-------------|------|------|\\n| FMVD (velocity dispersion) | 0.0825 | ❌ NO-GO | zero-cost 但无效 |\\n| K=2 L2 divergence | TBD (~1-2h) | ⏳ | 需要 K=2 采样，非 zero-cost |\\n\", \"total_chars\": 2132, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

    "content": "{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_8/projects/acr-vla-conformal-refinement/memory.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Project Memory\\\\n\\\\n## 失败模式记录\\\\n\\\\n- [失败模式] Cron false positive：eval 脚本不写 W&B 也不写 sentinel file，进程正常退出后 cron 5 次检测找不到标记 → 自动标记为 failed。受影响：eval-vla-only-long-seed46, eval-vla-only-goal-seed46。修复方案：tail 日志确认实际完成 → 手动修正 conclusion=success + 补录 metrics。预防：eval 实验应检查 output JSON 是否存在作为 sentinel。\\\\n\\\\n- [失败模式] FMVD 信号无效：ODE velocity dispersion 与 K=10 action variance 几乎无相关性 (ρ=0.0825, 远低于 0.3 阈值)。根因：flow matching 的确定性 ODE 轨迹的 velocity norm 变异编码的是 denoising dynamics，而非 action-level uncertainty。per-task 方向不一致 (milk ρ=-0.284)。FMVD 分布极窄 (std=0.040)，无法区分 uncertain vs confident steps。**教训：zero-cost signal 不能假设有效——denoising 空间的统计量不一定映射到 action 空间的 uncertainty。K=2 L2 divergence (直接在 action 空间测量) 是更直觉的替代方案。**\\\\n\\\\n- [失败模式] OpenVLA-7B eval timeout：OpenVLA-7B 每 episode ~77s（SmolVLA ~30s），500 episodes 需 ~10.7h。默认 timeout_hours=6 不够 → EXIT_CODE=124。修复方案：OpenVLA eval 实验 timeout_hours 至少设 12h。\\\\n\\\\n## LIBERO VLA-only Baselines (SmolVLA, K=1)\\\\n\\\\n### LIBERO-Goal (n=5)\\\\n| Seed | SR |\\\\n|------|------|\\\\n| seed42 | 71% |\\\\n| seed43 | 69% |\\\\n| seed44 | 73% |\\\\n| seed45 | 63% |\\\\n| seed46 | 71% |\\\\n| **Mean±Std** | **69.4%±3.8%** |\\\\n\\\\n### LIBERO-10 / Long (n=5)\\\\n| Seed | SR |\\\\n|------|------|\\\\n| seed42 | 11% |\\\\n| seed43 | 13% |\\\\n| seed44 | 12% |\\\\n| seed45 | 17% |\\\\n| seed46 | 18% |\\\\n| **Mean±Std** | **14.2%±3.1%** |\\\\n\\\\n## Spatial obs_only CRM (ε=0.2, K=10) Cross-seed Results (n=6)\\\\n\\\\n| Seed | SR |\\\\n|------|------|\\\\n| seed42 | 35% |\\\\n| seed43 | 34% |\\\\n| seed44 | 36% |\\\\n| seed45 | 34% |\\\\n| seed46 | 33% |\\\\n| seed47 | 32% |\\\\n| **Mean±Std** | **34.0%±1.4%** |\\\\n\\\\nSpatial VLA-only baseline: 29.3%±1.7% (n=4). The file has schema: {result: string} where result is a JSON string containing experiment entries.

Your task:
1. First probe the structure with jq to understand the format
2. Extract ALL experiments that have status=DONE or conclusion=success, focusing on their exp_id, mode, metrics (especially success_rate or similar), and any benchmark/suite info
3. Group experiments by benchmark and mode:
   - LIBERO-Object: exp_id containing "object"
   - LIBERO-Spatial: exp_id containing "spatial" 
   - LIBERO-Goal: exp_id containing "goal"
   - LIBERO-Long: exp_id containing "long"
   
   And by mode:
   - CRM obs_only: mode containing "crm" or exp_id containing "crm" or "obs-only"
   - VLA-only: mode containing "vla_only" or exp_id containing "vla-only"
   - Argmin: exp_id containing "argmin"
   - Adaptive K: exp_id containing "adaptive"

4. For each group, extract the success_rate values (as floats)

5. Write a Python script that:
   - Uses the extracted data (hardcoded arrays)
   - Also incorporates these known data points if registry is incomplete:
     * CRM-Spatial seeds: [0.32, 0.34, 0.36, 0.34, 0.34, 0.33, 0.32, 0.35] (n=8)
     * VLA-Spatial seeds: [0.30, 0.29, 0.31, 0.27, 0.30, 0.30, 0.30, 0.30, 0.29] (n=9)
     * CRM-Long: [0.17, 0.15, 0.20, 0.18, 0.18, 0.17] (n=6)
     * VLA-Long: [0.11, 0.13, 0.12, 0.17, 0.18, 0.14, 0.11] (n=7)
     * CRM-Goal: [0.67, 0.72, 0.64, 0.65, 0.72] (n=5)
     * VLA-Goal: [0.71, 0.69, 0.73, 0.63, 0.71] (n=5)
   - Computes mean ± std for each group
   - Computes paired or independent t-test (scipy.stats.ttest_ind) for CRM vs VLA-only
   - Also computes Mann-Whitney U test
   - Prints all results in a structured format

6. Run the Python script and return the complete output

Be thorough in extracting data from the registry. Look at ALL experiments. Pay special attention to success_rate in metrics fields. Print all exp_ids you find with their success rates so we can verify completeness.

Independent two-sample t-test (Welch's, unequal variance)\n    t_stat, t_pval = stats.ttest_ind(a, b, equal_var=False)\n\n    # Mann-Whitney U test (non-parametric)\n    u_stat, u_pval = stats.mannwhitneyu(a, b, alternative='two-sided')\n\n    # Effect size: Cohen's d\n    pooled_std = np.sqrt(((len(a)-1)*a.std(ddof=1)**2 + (len(b)-1)*b.std(ddof=1)**2) /\n                         (len(a) + len(b) - 2))\n    cohens_d = (a.mean() - b.mean()) / pooled_std if pooled_std > 0 else float('inf')\n\n    # Difference\n    diff = a.mean() - b.mean()\n    diff_pct = diff * 100  # percentage points\n\n    print(f\"\\n  {name_a} vs {name_b}:\")\n    print(f\"    Difference: {diff:+.4f} ({diff_pct:+.2f} pp)\")\n    print(f\"    Welch's t-test: t={t_stat:.4f}, p={t_pval:.6f} {'***' if t_pval < 0.001 else '**' if t_pval < 0.01 else '*' if t_pval < 0.05 else 'n.s.'}\")\n    print(f\"    Mann-Whitney U: U={u_stat:.1f}, p={u_pval:.6f} {'***' if u_pval < 0.001 else '**' if u_pval < 0.01 else '*' if u_pval < 0.05 else 'n.s.'}\")\n    print(f\"    Cohen's d: {cohens_d:.4f} ({'large' if abs(cohens_d) > 0.8 else 'medium' if abs(cohens_d) > 0.5 else 'small' if abs(cohens_d) > 0.2 else 'negligible'})\")\n\n    return t_pval, u_pval\n\n# ============================================================================\n# DATA FROM REGISTRY (verified from all experiments with conclusion=success)\n# ============================================================================\n\n# --- LIBERO-Spatial ---\n# CRM obs_only (norm02 variants, K=5 and K=10): seeds 42-48\n# From registry: seed42=0.35, seed43=0.32(k5) or 0.34(norm02-r2), seed44=0.37(k5-r2) or 0.36(norm02),\n# seed45=0.34(k5-r2) or 0.34(norm02), seed46=0.33(r2), seed47=0.32(norm02), seed48=0.35(norm02 and crm-spatial)\n# Using the best-matched K=10 norm02 experiments (primary config):\ncrm_spatial = [0.34, 0.36, 0.34, 0.33, 0.32, 0.35]  # Here is the complete analysis output. The script is saved at `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_8/projects/acr-vla-conformal-refinement/artifacts/statistical_analysis.py`.

**Key findings from the registry (402 total experiments, 89 with success_rate metrics):**

**LIBERO-Spatial** (CRM +4.6 pp, p<0.001): CRM obs_only significantly outperforms VLA-only (0.341 vs 0.296). Very large effect size (Cohen's d=3.49).

**LIBERO-Long** (CRM +3.4 pp, p=0.016): CRM obs_only significantly outperforms VLA-only (0.175 vs 0.141). Large effect size (d=1.54).

**LIBERO-Goal** (CRM -1.4 pp, p=0.579): No significant difference. VLA-only actually slightly higher (0.694 vs 0.680), but well within noise.

**LIBERO-Object** (CRM +6.9 pp, p<0.001): CRM obs_only (norm01) strongly outperforms VLA-only (0.175 vs 0.106). Massive effect size (d=5.15). This holds across all CRM configs (norm01, norm02 K=10, norm02 K=5).

**Argmin vs VLA** on Object: Not significant (p=0.255), only +1.8 pp improvement.

**CRM vs Argmin** on Object: CRM norm01 significantly outperforms Argmin K=10 (p=0.014), +5.1 pp.

**Adaptive-K** on Object: Significantly better than VLA-only (p<0.001, +3.5 pp) but significantly worse than CRM norm01 (p<0.001, -3.4 pp). It sits between VLA-only and CRM.
